## Shared production lock descriptor context — production, 22 October 2026

- Operations source commit `1752360e22c194a6f02d1e5269d98abc8189c446` updates the tracked shared-lock wrapper to export `SALEMAX_DEPLOY_LOCK_FD=9` after acquiring the existing OS-level lock. This lets production preflight and object-storage migration tooling verify that the calling process inherited descriptor 9 for the exact shared lock inode. The shared helper `/opt/salemax/shared/with-production-lock.sh` is mode `0700`, owner UID:GID `1001:1001`, SHA-256 `fc2dc997b3a51b6d8212a0219c67820eb144f27f98e99d8653e61083c772fd4e`. The active application release remains `/opt/salemax/releases/follow-up-message-4f7ed6d-20261022`; this update does not deploy or identify a new application release.
- One synchronous SSH process invoked the existing helper, held lock inode `2049:850068` continuously, re-resolved the active release after acquisition, checked host `carsqatar-prod-01`, verified the prior helper hash, and atomically installed the reviewed source file. The competing nonblocking flock returned 75 while the operation was still running. The new descriptor marker and `/proc` descriptor device/inode matched the shared lock. The lock was not replaced or unlinked.
- Protected rollback snapshot `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-lock-wrapper-fd9-1752360-20261022-retry1` (directory mode `0700`) contains the exact prior helper at SHA-256 `309c08e513320eda9634f7c23c523d530546e8e0fc747b7abc330bae64561c22` and a non-secret manifest. To roll back, run one synchronous SSH command through the shared helper and atomically restore `with-production-lock.sh` from that snapshot, preserving its owner and mode `0700`; do not replace or unlink the shared lock file. No migration or application rollback applies.
- Verification under the same lock confirmed the active release pointer was unchanged, all four `salemax` service IDs were unchanged (app `53d1ba6171e722166cd62f19d7b2291ad4edf653b56310dcb4db98397c7e4`, database `0a8f89195e0537bebe331c25d6df91f849ed968f3e030ec59e403402b68e7665`, recruitment notification worker `19b26d1df554adbbf5b90c32b0571c0d7a39eef42b870e8817eba1c930eb3d3e`, report worker `831faacd9c36fabaf90c8660f501848726eb5789bd354af0ec3980811e0fd87b`), and app/database remained healthy. No service, release, database, customer data, or migration changed. Root filesystem is 97% used with approximately 1.9 GiB free; inode use is 23%. No new browser/API acceptance is associated with this wrapper-only support change.
- The lock-context helper, migration guard, and hosted Linux concurrent-wrapper integration passed in ARM64 workflow run [39761725644](https://github.com/Ahammedsajal/SaleMax/actions/runs/39761725644) on source `ebf93a9ac5866a78393006863ec44bc6b2770cf5`; the wrapper change is present in this deployed source line. The workflow built and smoke-tested a non-root ARM64 image but did not publish it. The currently running app/worker image provenance and compatibility, disk remediation, alerts, backups/restore, representative load, authenticated bilingual acceptance, 7network.live migration, and the full 42-ticket program remain open.

## Primary-origin session and browser perimeter audit — read-only, 22 October 2026

- A follow-up public HTTPS probe against `7network.live`, `www.7network.live`, and `crm.salemax.qa` returned HTTP 200 without redirects and with valid TLS. Anonymous GETs did not set a session cookie. This confirms public reachability only, not authenticated session, API, provider, or device acceptance.

The current public server probe could not resolve the SSH alias `carsqatar-prod-01`, so the local bridge file hash was not compared with the running container in this follow-up.

Confirm HSTS policy and any legitimate cross-origin clients before changing headers. No production configuration, session, release, service, or customer data changed; no migration or rollback applies.

## API preflight ordering overlay — production, 22 October 2026

- Source commit `db7a5d6e53d76054e8c1422c2510d4982f3b1cd9` moves the existing app-wide `cors()` middleware ahead of the compatibility routers so preflight OPTIONS requests reach it before route authentication. It remains on app image ID `sha256:7c295567c1b42e1d76206b09cfd2261ccd6586d6fe91b9d0ea1439c2b85e5782`, requested as `salemax-app:follow-up-message-4f7ed6d-20261022`. The app remained healthy with zero restarts after a controlled restart. The database image remains `sha256:70cc072b29b4a89ae07abb2d4da2c64678a7f2dfe092751bb51c87d67dc1338b`; both workers remain on `sha256:d3731b54eb384aa433da59b66c0d7b6eda156d96104e10431ba0bbd1bd3ef915` requested as mutable `salemax-app`. No container image was rebuilt or replaced.
- The production `server.js` had live-only source differences from the operations worktree, so the reviewed middleware move was applied as a targeted overlay to that current file; no other live code was replaced. The active release and running container now both have `server.js` SHA-256 `9c5c9c3f7a0cedacf8516d825c5a08d5eb95cbbbf7384e9a93ece483b3c1fc51` (prior hash `7b5e3c341e7929dd165ad81bb05c4cfee25d7f2839a3c8a3da996aa4493471cd`). Private rollback snapshot `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-cors-preflight-db7a5d6e-20261022-retry3` is mode `0700`; exact prior release/container files and its manifest are mode `0600`. Restore those two files with their recorded owners/modes under the same shared lock, restart app container `53d1ba6171e7`, then verify health and the previous preflight rejection.
- Live checks now return HTTP 204 with the existing wildcard allow-origin and requested auth/content headers for `OPTIONS /api/user/training/tasks` from `crm.salemax.qa` to `7network.live` and from `7network.live` to `crm.salemax.qa`. A cross-origin unauthenticated GET remains HTTP 401 and includes the established CORS header. `https://7network.live/healthz` reports `status: ok`. No database migration or customer-data change occurred.
- This is a tracked live overlay, not an immutable image deployment: a future container recreation from the current image would discard the runtime-layer copy until the source change is incorporated into a published digest-pinned app image. The PR branch image workflow did not publish. Preserve this live change when producing the next app artifact and verify both app and worker artifact identities before rollout. Authenticated business browser journeys, English/Arabic, mobile, CSRF/session, sockets, provider callbacks, HSTS policy, and the full domain migration remain open. The root filesystem measured 97% used/1.9 GiB free before this operation and 90% used/4.9 GiB free afterward; this operation did not remove data, and the decrease is un-attributed rather than credited as cleanup.

## Inbox follow-up duplicate protection and task link — production, 22 October 2026

## Inbox and Team access SPA route cleanup — production, 14 October 2026

- Source commit: `a611a1b1e5a1edd1ee71930500af2ebef2c84617` on `main` (`client/public/team-invitations.js`).
- Deployed release: `/opt/salemax/releases/team-route-sync-a611a1b-20261014`; previous release: `/opt/salemax/releases/sales-team-access-focus-d8fad80-20261014`.
- Root cause: the maintained Team access script injected a fixed-position screen and removed it on `popstate`, but the existing React shell navigates between query routes with `history.pushState`. Selecting Inbox therefore left the Team access screen mounted over the inbox. The deployed script now calls its route update on `pushState`, `replaceState`, `popstate`, `hashchange`, and `pageshow`, removing the injected screen as soon as navigation leaves Team access.
- Database/data: no migrations and no customer, conversation, membership, or account data changes. No app container restart was needed. The source release and running app container serve the same updated JS and HTML.
- Rollback: protected baseline `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-team-route-sync-a611a1b-20261014` (mode 0700) contains the prior release path and prior/container copies of the script and HTML. Restore `/opt/salemax/current` to the previous release and copy both baseline files back into `salemax-app-1:/app/client/public/`; the earlier script cache reference is preserved in the baseline HTML.
- Verification: updated script passes `node --check` in the app container; release/container SHA-256 match for JS (`4cd5428a0ea92c0f4ac30c8393ca2c171c2723419f5a54475042da7006d3fb37`) and HTML (`dff1730966ca9a159e8554b57cdf98a5ebcfe5586c658847603829242abb4322)); `/healthz` returns success. Authenticated production browser acceptance loaded Team access, clicked Inbox, and confirmed URL `/user?page=inbox`, visible conversations, and no `#sx-team-screen` element. The Inbox header/list occupy the normal application content area.
- Remaining gates: no Arabic route acceptance or narrow/mobile viewport check was run. No other inbox behaviors were changed or revalidated.

- Deployed release: `/opt/salemax/releases/sales-lead-import-95affeef-20261014`; previous release: `/opt/salemax/releases/sales-owner-guard-2f4d578-20261014`. The existing Lead Pipeline now offers a SaleMaX Sales Business Admin CSV import. The form previews up to 100 leads; the API validates the batch, restricts access to the Sales owner, checks active same-tenant canonical agent assignments, rejects duplicates and writes leads plus activity history in one transaction. Existing scheduled calls, reports, follow-ups and the existing pipeline screen remain in place.
- No database migration was applied. No tenant, identity, staff, lead, call, sale, commission, invoice, receipt or other business record was created or changed. Production still has zero `salemax_sales` tenants and zero `admin@salemax.qa` identities.
- App image `sha256:5b8a329885c7b8096edfb28195ea6ece473a39a1f30afafb87e8b065d3f12efc`; app container `dbad7a96e6d81e95fad04532a25ab60abff2829189452085062e96d0b882df9b`, healthy with zero restarts. Database container `0a8f89195e0537bebe331c25d6df91f849ed968f3e030ec59e403402b68e7665` and report-worker container `4e0fa5e8b03d8d79a116bdb7691be7004e458adec3670230349595c0f43d5eaa` retained their IDs and start times. Filesystem availability after the build-cache prune and deployment is 2.0 GB.
- Rollback: protected baseline `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-sales-lead-import-95affeef-20261014` (mode 0700; baseline file mode 0600) records the prior release and container IDs. The previous image is retained as `salemax-app:rollback-sales-lead-import-95affeef-20261014`. Restore `/opt/salemax/current` to `/opt/salemax/releases/sales-owner-guard-2f4d578-20261014`, retag the rollback image as `salemax-app:latest`, then recreate only the app with `docker compose -p salemax --env-file /opt/salemax/shared/stack.env -f /opt/salemax/current/deploy/compose.yml up -d --no-build --no-deps --force-recreate app`. No database rollback applies.
- Verification: unit checks pass 5/5. Disposable MariaDB 11.4 Sales integration passed with 91 migrations, including transactional CSV assignment/duplicate rejection, commission payout batches, Finance and organization role scope. Source JS syntax and diff checks pass. Production `/healthz` and the Pipeline HTML/CSS/JS return HTTP 200; served asset SHA-256 values match source, release and running image: HTML `6cb37303beba3643d01172f9232529cbd7d0f2d62f68c92a33d17e90992e16bc`, CSS `bb84267bd558c0d75c92f4031bff11b061b5d4ae0cc94aacfdd285a261a0d9f9`, JS `826ae88827b8f18a3e77ba9c0de132f5e4b7023df022a4b9aaee10bb520840f1`. ## AI-written Hybrid course replies — 11 October 2026

- A clear follow-up about one matched course now uses the configured AI only to phrase a short, natural answer. The model receives that course's approved live business facts, gets at most 90 output tokens, and must cite the matched course source; the existing answer validator checks citations, prices, numbers, links and commitments before delivery. Menus and exact fee/duration replies remain deterministic and token-free.

The normal handover behavior remains in place for other questions.

## Tenant CRM DNS lifecycle hardening — production, 10 October 2026

- Source commits `f441c8186642f95d8d79743cf7dd4c7698b8fe00` and `79e618af0ea68aa54944367dfb3e42e255b837fb` are on `Ahammedsajal/SaleMax` main. This hardens the existing `/user` CRM domain feature with public-suffix-aware subdomain validation, a persistent DNS-check cooldown, active-domain DNS health state and transition audit events, a 15-minute systemd DNS audit, fail-closed routing for stale DNS or suspended tenants, and retirement checks that require both tenant disablement and removal of SaleMaX DNS. TLS activation rechecks TXT and CNAME ownership.

Full feature rollback can use that initial previous multiple-course image/release.
- Configuration: Training BOT is live Hybrid, revision 9, assigned to exactly the intended connected test channel and restricted to the single explicitly authorized test recipient. The previous Guided profile is paused at revision 18 and unassigned. OpenAI and the required privacy acknowledgement were verified saved. Existing manager handover routing remains configured; the test conversation was restored to inherited bot mode, control revision 8. Temporary keyword verification rules were removed. No provider secrets, customer histories or private channel identifiers are stored in Git.
- Verification: 217/217 focused checks passed locally and in the exact Linux production image. The dedicated disposable MariaDB integration applied/replayed 73 migrations and proved tenant/permission boundaries, profile revisions and privacy/provider controls, unique assignment, pause/resume, provider failure handoff, duplicate inbound suppression, and 12 concurrent QR-echo writes yielding one stored provider message. The broader unrelated business-contract integration stops at its existing delivery-column assertion; no whole-repository integration pass is claimed.
- Live acceptance: actual authenticated Inbox sends on the approved phone pair exercised English/Arabic facts, comparisons, registration links, course follow-ups, editable keyword replies and handover. The user confirmed replies were visible and readable on the receiving WhatsApp device. MS Office/Excel fee replies and an honest missing-duration reply recorded 0 input/output AI tokens. Real human-request handover paused the chat, retained an existing open staff task, and the next incoming fee question produced no automated reply; resume restored replies. A deliberately disallowed runtime recipient returned handled/paused before claiming a turn or sending anything; no message was transmitted to another number. Provider-ID echo deduplication was also observed on real messages. A live pure-AI preview used the same current business source; AI mode was not assigned as an additional live bot.
- Live cost proof: a location answer used 888 input + 47 output tokens, and repeating it through the same saved-bot preview returned the identical answer with cacheHit=true and 0 + 0 tokens. Final measured WhatsApp AI turns used 1248–1489 input and 66–129 output tokens; multi-course/Arabic context exceeds the typical 600–1200 input target in some cases. Output is capped at 250 tokens, compact history at four messages, retrieval at three relevant courses, and provider input at 12 KB. Test usage reached 42,869 consumed / 0 reserved tokens against the unchanged 50,000 daily limit. This is the application's token accounting, not a provider billing invoice or a model price comparison.
- Authenticated production UI passed English/Arabic at 1440px and 390px: existing Hybrid editor, privacy confirmation, editable short fee/missing-duration wording and conversational preview with token metrics; no horizontal overflow. Screenshots and detailed logs are outside Git in the operator verification directory. The isolated test database/container and temporary server environment files were removed after verification; protected rollback material remains.
- Data/acceptance limits: Advanced Excel has no published duration, so the bot says so; it never invents one. Unknown refund/discount policies require an approved business FAQ or staff confirmation. No real registration, payment or financial action was submitted during chatbot testing. Provider/network outage and concurrent SQL contracts have synthetic coverage; not every failure was induced against production. This release improves human tone and grounded behavior, but does not assert universal model accuracy, perfect human equivalence, a live Gemini price comparison, or completion of the broader 42-ticket upgrade.

- Source is merged to `main` through PRs #149, #150, #151 and #152. Final source/deployment-record base commit: `e943ba33fd74cabeb761987f530c37cbf85e3f08`. - Migration `20261207_qr_channel_shares.sql` is applied (production migration ledger: 92 total, 3/3 statements). It adds QR origin-channel identity to chats, a bounded-prefix lookup index, and tenant/member/channel/actor-scoped QR share records. The first index attempt was rejected before applying any statement because legacy `beta_chats.uid` is too wide for an unprefixed compound key; the migration was corrected to use 191-character index prefixes and then applied successfully. The pre-migration database backup is `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-qr-channel-sharing-b1a85e0-20261014/pre-migration.sql.gz`, SHA-256 `299c064979c50e3a2c4e0707bcd9e97e78d6b89661311181359f972c0d57b818`.

The owner Team access screen shows the connected number assigned to ProCatalyst Manager and 33 chats at initial acceptance. The live Manager-scope check after final rollout found one shared QR connection and 34 matching chats. The Manager sidebar response includes the QR section through both role navigation and any saved sidebar allowlist; the QR APIs still filter to assigned connections, and setup/write permissions remain unchanged.
- Manager Inbox scope resolves to the verified tenant owner’s existing QR session. Runtime verification confirmed that a reply request is authorized against the persisted shared-channel chat record, ignoring a forged client UID. The existing socket and QR ingestion paths persist the channel identity and notify both owner and Manager inbox scopes. No WhatsApp message was sent during this release, so a new live provider inbound/reply round trip remains unverified.
- Verification: focused checks pass 11/11 (`team-invitations`, `team-inbox-scope`, `team-open-chat-access`); production Manager-scope verification reports QR navigation visible in role and saved navigation, one shared connection, 34 chats, and persisted-chat reply authorization. `https://crm.salemax.qa/healthz` returned `success:true,status:ok`; `/user/login` returned HTTP 200; app container is healthy with zero restarts. Owner browser acceptance reconfirmed the assigned Manager and connected QR status. No authenticated Manager browser session was available for a visual sign-in check.
- Rollback: protected directory `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-qr-share-nav-e943ba3-20261014` retains the immediately previous release path, previous app image ID, and prior `team-invitations.js`; rollback image tag is `salemax-app:rollback-qr-share-nav-e943ba3-20261014`. Restore the prior release/image and recreate only `app`. For a database rollback, use the separate pre-migration backup above only with a reviewed maintenance-window recovery plan; the QR-share rollback migration is `database/migrations/20261207_qr_channel_shares_rollback.sql`. The manager assignment remains active unless explicitly revoked through Team access.
- Remaining acceptance: verify the Manager’s authenticated QR page and a controlled new inbound/reply cycle on an approved test conversation. No message was sent to a customer as part of this deployment.

## Finance dashboard and account-controlled daily QR reporting - deployed 16 October 2026

- Source d0b0f62aa99f06f6ddcde186125724a07a5dd931, PR #161, replaces the Reports route's invoice-register rendering with a dedicated dashboard. Qatar date presets/manual dates, clickable sales/receipts/receivable/overdue cards, monthly charts, collection health, installment aging and paginated linked records are included. Generated documents are deduplicated against posted invoices; archived/replaced documents are excluded. Existing allocations, reversals, credits, excess deposits and sales-agent payment credit retain their accounting behavior. Managers gain read access while invoice/payment writes remain restricted.
- Each owner configures and verifies their own WhatsApp recipients and activates/pauses a daily Qatar-time schedule directly on Finance Reports. The suggested time is 23:59 and a new schedule defaults to paused. Deployment created zero recipients and zero schedules and sent no messages. QR transport uses the existing protected internal service and a verified tenant recipient, refuses ambiguous senders, and records unknown send outcomes without automatic retransmission. Meta template transport remains available when QR mode is not selected.
- Active release: /opt/salemax/releases/finance-dashboard-d0b0f62-20261016. Prior release: /opt/salemax/releases/receipt-combobox-4c60815-20261016. Fifteen reviewed files were overlaid while preserving all other current live invoice/receipt changes and the production-generated index. Live baseline hashes were checked immediately before deployment. This is a reproducible source patch over the recorded live baseline, not a clean-source reconstruction of the legacy image.
- App and report worker use immutable image sha256:6b11d2c7b2d67b7bdfbe549bbf577710e9d9d71aadd0cde90969e0f5dbadba9f, tagged salemax-app:finance-dashboard-d0b0f62 and salemax-app:latest. The image extends the unchanged app image sha256:c9a2acad19d70b030118a418db022f6bbf340a914d467af6887a8c5481bfef94; no runtime/customer files were imported into the build. Only app and report-worker were recreated, at 2026-10-16T19:35:10Z (22:35 Qatar); both have zero restarts. Database container/start time was unchanged. There are no migrations or financial/customer record mutations in this release.
- Protected runtime configuration enables QR report transport and delivery capability, uses the app Docker service as the internal origin, and supplies strong shared internal-service and verification secrets. Secret values remain only in protected server runtime files. No account can send until its owner activates a schedule and verifies a destination. One existing training tenant has a connected sender and reports both verification/delivery readiness; the tenant without a sender correctly reports unavailable.
- Verification: six new tests pass. Disposable MariaDB checks cover generated/posted deduplication, archived/replaced exclusion, partial payments, pending exclusion, reversals, credits, installment aging, receipt/application links, monthly totals, manager read, tenant isolation, paging and Qatar midnight. Synthetic browser checks pass in English/Arabic at 1440/390/260px, including cards/presets, invoice/receipt details, aging, pagination and recipient verification. Full suite: 764/789 pass; the same 25 failing names are present in unchanged origin/main (758/783). No new failure was introduced.
- Live authenticated owner API reads pass for all four detail views on both training tenants, plus generated invoice detail, recipient settings and schedule settings. Three existing invoice/receivable/overdue records and the empty second tenant were verified without exposing customer details in tracked evidence. Daily snapshots build with activity, journey, finance, dashboard totals, comparisons and action items for both tenants, without queuing/sending. Worker-to-app protected QR read returns 200. Anonymous requests disclose no report data. All 15 container file hashes and all four public assets match the reviewed overlay; /healthz returns 200.
- Remaining account-controlled acceptance: actual recipient verification, an owner-activated schedule and real WhatsApp delivery. No test message was sent, no real recipient/schedule was added, and no full authenticated production-browser mutation was performed. Current balances are selected-invoice-cohort balances, not historical closing balances. QR transport requires exactly one active sender for the account.
- Rollback: /opt/salemax/shared/rollback-finance-dashboard-d0b0f62 retains the restricted prior runtime file, prior container configuration, baseline/updated source manifest and previous release pointer. The previous app image is also tagged salemax-app:rollback-finance-dashboard-d0b0f62; prior worker image is sha256:0809853aed633ddcec3ced0b857b401ccc76c81c3888b502f4aadc90eaecb1b7. Restore prior runtime and release, use the exact prior app/worker images, and recreate only those two services. No database rollback is required. If users have subsequently configured schedules, disable delivery before rollback and preserve their recipient/schedule records. A full restore was not rehearsed in production.
- Evidence, screenshots, deployment scripts, source manifest and public acceptance results remain outside Git at A:/Salemax - Training center/verification/finance-dashboard-20261016. The broader 42-ticket program is not marked complete by this release.

## SaleMaX generated invoice detail compatibility — 17 October 2026

- Source commit `2b3b601` (`Open generated invoices through legacy detail route`) adds a read-only compatibility fallback to the existing user and accountant formal invoice detail APIs. If a tenant-scoped formal invoice lookup returns `INVOICE_NOT_FOUND`, the API attempts the generated-invoice payment context under the same authorized tenant. ## Meta WABA subscription on manual connection — production, 18 October 2026

- Source commit `37a9ce8` is pushed to `Ahammedsajal/SaleMax` `main`. The manual Meta connection now calls `POST /{WABA_ID}/subscribed_apps` with the saved tenant token after the WABA phone number is validated and credentials are saved. The token is sent only in the Bearer authorization header; provider error payloads and credentials are not returned or logged. A failed Graph subscription is reported distinctly so operators can retry. The route requires the WABA ID, access token, phone-number ID and app ID. ## English-first public website and connected sales enquiries — production, 20 October 2026

- Implementation `cdbc6a105b35e1cefa83dfb26b28fccf4b5be164`, overlay-permission support `6a8d2d1`, and acceptance fixes `31ee5d7`, `ed1992c`, `ae5b885` are pushed to `Ahammedsajal/SaleMax` main. - Public intake creates the existing admin `contact_form` record and a first-party Sales opportunity through the existing Pipeline service in one transaction. Tenant/owner selection is server-controlled and requires the active verified first-party Sales owner; no roles, memberships, plans or customer accounts were expanded. Admin contact deletion remains independent of the Sales lead lifecycle. No notification, provider campaign or payment is initiated by the new form.

Tenant/team scope and indexed chat columns are retained. ## Recruitment application CV storage ownership repair — production, 20 October 2026

- Confirmed the public Sales recruitment submission failed with `EACCES` while creating its private CV directory. The app runs as UID/GID `1000:1000`, but the mounted storage root `/opt/salemax/shared/private/recruitment-cvs` was owned by `1001:1001` with mode `0700`. The failed transaction rolled back; the public form presented its generic validation message.
- Runtime-only repair: changed that existing storage root to owner/group `1000:1000`, retaining mode `0700`. No application source, schema, provider configuration, or candidate data was changed. No migration or app restart was required. This deployment-record commit is the Git source record for the operational repair; app code was not redeployed by this task.
- The release when repaired was `/opt/salemax/releases/salemax-recruitment-dual-b1d8395-20261020`. A concurrent, independent release switched the active pointer to `/opt/salemax/releases/salemax-sidebar-mobile-overlay-655b2c0-20261020` before verification; the shared storage fix remains effective. Verified running image `sha256:7bec1ae7e655fb9a08ec9b24e9e2813822f2970eb86cd0ce8446bfacf00f03ac`, healthy with zero restarts. Recruitment service SHA-256 `189fed145ccb780b0327dc01910cf7e5dc3cea204da7b91c86e921a8a5d213d3`; router `fa212cef54e457d1732d4eb88144e1c1d040f56a8f871cff62d1baaf5a7f917f`.
- Verification: app UID can write the private storage root. A disposable synthetic PDF and candidate exercised the deployed `createPublic` service, including CV writing, application insert, audit event, and founder notification queue insert. A diagnostic connection wrapper deliberately rejected the commit and confirmed transaction rollback; the service removed the synthetic CV and the temporary upload was deleted. No synthetic application remains and no notification was committed or sent. Public health and careers page return HTTP 200. The storage root remains `0700` owned by `1000:1000` after the independent release.
- Backup/rollback: `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-recruitment-cv-permissions-20261020` (mode `0700`) contains the original numeric ownership/mode and release pointer. For exact metadata rollback, restore owner/group `1001:1001` and mode `0700` on the storage root; doing so reinstates the diagnosed submission failure. No database restore applies.
- Remaining gates: a committed applicant submission, browser English/Arabic acceptance, owner CV download, and provider delivery were not exercised by this repair. The existing generic UI error remains. Future storage provisioning must use the runtime container UID/GID and preserve private modes. The full 42-ticket objective remains in progress.

## Shared custom-CRM monthly/yearly plans — production, 20 October 2026

The public pricing section displays exactly two paid plans from the existing catalogue: ID 26 **Monthly CRM**, QAR 350 / 30 days; ID 25 **Yearly CRM**, QAR 3,700 / 365 days. The existing trial remains in Manage Plans/history and is omitted from the two public paid-plan cards. ## SaleMaX Sales recruitment application reliability — production, 21 October 2026

- Source commit `82376165e227a09fecefc522061a33a5f8b73e97` is pushed to `Ahammedsajal/SaleMax` `main`. The public form now shows applicants a clear English or Arabic action message and the safe application error code in brackets. Saving an application and CV no longer depends on successfully enqueueing the optional Founder notification; queue errors are logged without exposing candidate data or rolling back the application.
- Root cause: application persistence and Founder notification enqueue shared one transaction. A notification enqueue/database error therefore rolled back an otherwise valid application and CV and surfaced as HTTP 503. Nginx retained seven 503 submit responses on 20 October between 09:51:36 and 09:52:08 UTC, but the historical app log did not preserve their downstream error code. The reliability defect is fixed regardless of whether the queue is temporarily unavailable.
- Active release `/opt/salemax/releases/salemax-recruitment-apply-8237616-20261021`, based on `/opt/salemax/releases/base-crm-5ed3432-20261021`; app image `sha256:8027c4fa67db12fbd2208cab78cddf27e692b353f1e6920ba7cd4da2953b7f18`. Exactly three files changed: application HTML, public application JavaScript and the recruitment application service. The generated production shell was retained. No migrations or application/customer data changes occurred.
- Rollback `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-recruitment-apply-8237616-20261021` preserves the previous release/image, Compose file, service baseline and exact pre-change runtime files. Previous release `/opt/salemax/releases/base-crm-5ed3432-20261021`; previous image `sha256:64b4ef8d498c53be8da7f0dbfb2b40df2c3ea14cc927a30dee079d4386c94da1`, tagged `salemax-app:rollback-recruitment-apply-8237616-20261021`. To roll back, point `/opt/salemax/current` to the previous release, tag the rollback image as `salemax-app:latest`, and recreate only the app with the previous release Compose file and protected `/opt/salemax/shared/stack.env`. No database rollback applies.
- Verification: recruitment/application, bilingual-copy, notification and chatbot tests pass 29/29; syntax and diff checks pass. A synthetic full transaction against the live database and a temporary CV directory returned a reference and was deliberately rolled back; no record or file persisted. After deployment, the public careers page and cache-busted script returned HTTP 200, and a honeypot multipart upload returned the expected HTTP 200 without creating an application. `/healthz` is healthy, the app has zero restarts, and release/container/HTTPS file hashes match. Database, report worker and recruitment notification worker IDs/start times are unchanged. No real candidate submitted an application during verification.

## Base CRM contract and existing-panel setup — production, 21 October 2026

All six container hashes match. Production-only content, generated shell structure and compiled bundle remain preserved. No migration or customer/user/membership/task/FAQ/bot/plan/configuration write occurs. Only app is recreated; database/report/recruitment workers retain identities/start times.
- Postflight: healthy app with zero restarts, HTTPS health/admin/business logins 200; served Manage Users script hash and generated cache loader match. Registry resolves base_crm v1 with no catalogue and public:false. Tenant counts remain Training Center v1 two and Sales v1 one; Base CRM plan-version count remains zero. Migration ledger remains 113 applied. No synthetic customer was created in production merely for verification.

## Tenant custom-field search in the existing Pipeline — production, 21 October 2026

- Source commits `dc3adeb` and `ecac7413a4b834a28751c693fffa093aa47b3470` are pushed to `Ahammedsajal/SaleMax` `main`. The initial overlay changed exactly the lead service and two Pipeline assets; the index follow-up changed only `helper/pipeline/leadPipeline.js`.
- Rollback snapshot `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-pipeline-custom-search-indexed-ecac741-20261021` (mode `0700`) preserves the prior release pointer, app image, Compose file, and exact previous `leadPipeline.js` preimage. Previous release `/opt/salemax/releases/pipeline-custom-search-dc3adeb-20261021`; previous image `sha256:14ab719c20f6585c9a8f617afa107688c71a33c234d4e300257d3d942e24ec65`, tagged `salemax-app:rollback-pipeline-custom-search-indexed-ecac741-20261021`. To roll back, restore the pointer to the previous release, retag the saved image as `salemax-app:latest`, and recreate only the app with the previous release Compose file and protected environment. No database rollback applies.
- Verification: focused custom-field search, Pipeline team-scope and Sales behavior tests pass 10/10; source/client syntax and `git diff --check` pass. MariaDB 11.4.13 returned text/integer/boolean JSON field values from the intended path. Read-only production aggregates show six businesses with Pipeline data (median 10 leads, maximum 242). For the largest current tenant, EXPLAIN changed from a small-table full scan (398 estimated rows) to `ref` on `idx_pipeline_board` (242 estimated tenant rows), followed by tenant-owned contact lookup and bounded JSON_TABLE projection (estimated 40 field rows per lead). The careers/pipeline page and cache-busted Pipeline script return HTTP 200; release, container and HTTPS script hashes match. `/healthz` is healthy; anonymous Pipeline API returns 401. Database, report worker and recruitment notification worker IDs/start times are unchanged. No migration or customer/tenant data changes occurred. Disk use is 91% (4.5 GiB free).
- The full legacy unit command did not reach a final summary after more than three minutes; it emitted stale admin-screen fixture failures and idle test processes, so it was stopped. Authenticated existing-screen/role acceptance and large-account latency measurements remain open. The JSON projection is a bounded first step; add a tenant/lead/field-key/value projection with transactional write-through and backfill if measured query plans or latency exceed the budget. The full category customization and 42-ticket upgrade remains in progress; real-estate, clinical, retail, hospitality and restaurant workflows are not delivered by this search change.

App is healthy with zero restarts; all other services remain running. No authenticated owner/staff browser session or mobile-device acceptance was performed. ## Agent Inbox quick reply authorization — production, 22 October 2026

- Source commit `e7781c795f3c0a76eb3670e926867b15aea2e04e` is pushed to `Ahammedsajal/SaleMax` `main`. The user reported a recurring generic 403 while signed into the Agent Inbox. Production access logs correlated it to `GET /api/user/get_all_quick_reply`: the shared legacy route classifier incorrectly treated this Inbox read as template management, and the Agent guard also rejected its narrow assigned scope because the legacy mount was not on the resource-aware list.
- The existing endpoint now requires `conversations.read` and may use the Agent's assigned scope for this exact GET. Quick reply creation/deletion and other `/api/user` endpoints remain protected by their existing rules. Focused route authorization coverage asserts read allowed and write denied.
- Active release `/opt/salemax/releases/agent-inbox-quick-replies-e7781c7-20261022` was cloned from `/opt/salemax/releases/agent-login-no-flash-d7c684f-20261022`; app image `salemax-app:agent-inbox-quick-replies-e7781c7-20261022`, immutable ID `sha256:a4c532845e757fa532c57661272cd6c1f1a3bf64f516d0688d46ee8d2f053a1b`. The production-generated shell and all unrelated files were retained. The active production `team-role-request-guard.js` had one live-only difference from tracked baseline (its unused properties route mapping was absent); that live baseline was retained and amended only for this permission fix. Deployed hashes: `agent-workspace-access.js` `7683cebe5d953adc504d5dba339193f9818ff5533985d26354ab8df523227fd2`; live-derived `team-role-request-guard.js` `aa106fb767cdd5df515f9ed77cf3922b650a416d98ca9c84759532e4900e26e9`.
- No migration or user, agent, conversation, or task data write occurred. Only `app` was recreated under Compose project `salemax`; MariaDB, report worker, and recruitment notification worker retained their container IDs and start times.
- Rollback snapshot `/opt/salemax/shared/rollback-agent-inbox-quick-replies-e7781c7-20261022` (mode `0700`) contains the prior release pointer, Compose file, exact preimage of both runtime files, app image ID/tag, service baseline, and hashes. Prior release is `/opt/salemax/releases/agent-login-no-flash-d7c684f-20261022`; prior app image `sha256:74ead8784000d42c3b3457c81889a117179fceea713eb20d1ccf4b75cdf1148e` is tagged `salemax-app:rollback-agent-inbox-quick-replies-e7781c7-20261022`. To roll back, point `/opt/salemax/current` to the prior release and recreate only `app` with `docker compose -p salemax -f /opt/salemax/current/deploy/compose.yml --env-file /opt/salemax/shared/stack.env up -d --no-deps app`; no database rollback applies.
- Verification: focused Agent workspace, Inbox scope, and team-access tests pass 25/25; source syntax and diff checks pass. A disposable container assertion confirms the GET resolves to `conversations.read` with `assigned` scope and the quick-reply write remains denied. After release, the same signed-in Agent browser reloads `/user?page=inbox` with the conversation list visible and no 403 toast. The correlated access log changed from 403 to HTTP 200 for the quick-reply GET. HTTPS `/user?page=inbox` and local `/healthz` return HTTP 200; the app is healthy with zero restarts. Database and both workers remained unchanged. No WhatsApp/provider message was sent.
- Remaining acceptance is limited to other Agent Inbox actions and broader bilingual/mobile checks; this change addresses the observed quick-reply authorization denial only.
